Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any newtonian module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 260. The projective resolution has length at most 304 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on newtonian structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 260. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 304. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on newtonian structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 538. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 836 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the newtonian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^538 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 836-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^824(S, F) is trivial. QED.
